<style>
.rt { --s1: #2a78d6; --s2: #eb6834; --s3: #1baf7a; --grid: rgba(128,128,128,.22); --muted: rgba(128,128,128,.95); font-size: 14px; }
@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { .rt { --s1: #3987e5; --s2: #d95926; --s3: #199e70; } }
.rt-bar { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-bottom: 6px; }
.rt-bar select { font-size: 15px; padding: 3px 6px; }
.rt-bar button { font-size: 13px; padding: 2px 9px; cursor: pointer; }
.rt-name { font-weight: 700; }
.rt-sub { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }
.rt-tabs { display: flex; gap: 4px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin: 8px 0 4px; }
.rt-tabs button { font-size: 13px; padding: 3px 12px; border-radius: 999px; border: 1px solid var(--grid);
  background: transparent; color: inherit; cursor: pointer; }
.rt-tabs button.on { background: var(--s1); border-color: var(--s1); color: #fff; }
.rt-legend { display: flex; gap: 14px; font-size: 12px; min-height: 18px; }
.rt-legend i { display: inline-block; width: 14px; height: 3px; border-radius: 2px; vertical-align: middle; margin-right: 5px; }
.rt-chart { position: relative; }
.rt-chart svg { width: 100%; height: auto; display: block; }
.rt-chart text { fill: currentColor; font-size: 11px; }
.rt-empty { padding: 40px 0; text-align: center; color: var(--muted); }
.rt-tip { position: absolute; pointer-events: none; display: none; background: rgba(20,24,22,.92); color: #fff;
  font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.45; padding: 6px 9px; border-radius: 6px; white-space: nowrap; }
.rt-wrap { overflow-x: auto; }
table.rt-t { border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 12px; margin-top: 10px; white-space: nowrap; }
table.rt-t th, table.rt-t td { padding: 2px 9px 2px 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--grid); text-align: left;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
table.rt-t th { font-weight: 600; }
table.rt-t td.neg { color: #c0392b; }
table.rt-t .hl { font-weight: 700; }
.rt-split { display: none; font-size: 12px; margin: 2px 0 0; cursor: pointer; }
.rt-notes { font-size: 13px; margin-top: .8em; padding-left: 1.2em; }
.rt-notes li { margin: 1px 0; }
</style>

<div class="rt">
<div class="rt-bar">
  <button id="rt-prev" title="Ctrl+←">◀</button>
  <select id="rt-sel"></select>
  <button id="rt-next" title="Ctrl+→">▶</button>
  <span class="rt-name" id="rt-name"></span>
  <span class="rt-sub" id="rt-sub"></span>
</div>

<label class="rt-split" id="rt-split"><input type="checkbox" id="rt-split-box" checked> Adjust for stock splits</label>

<div class="rt-wrap"><table class="rt-t" id="rt-t"></table></div>
<ul class="rt-notes">
<li><b>Gross profit margin</b> = gross profit ÷ revenue</li>
<li><b>Net profit margin</b> = net income ÷ revenue</li>
<li><b>Current ratio</b> = current assets ÷ current liabilities, at quarter end</li>
<li><b>OCF</b> = net cash from operating activities</li>
<li><b>Free cash flow (FCF)</b> = OCF − capital expenditures; top panel: OCF and capex bars, bottom panel: FCF line, same quarters</li>
<li><b>Shares outstanding</b> = all share classes on the 10-Q/10-K cover page, as of a date shortly after quarter end</li>
<li>Share counts before a stock split are restated to the post-split basis (e.g., NVDA 10-for-1, first seen in 24Q3); untick "Adjust for stock splits" to see them as filed</li>
<li>Splits are found where the cover count jumps by a standard ratio (×2, ×10, ÷20, …) between quarters, and are listed next to the company name</li>
<li><b>Public float</b> = market value of shares held by non-affiliates, reported once a year in the 10-K as of the end of the second fiscal quarter</li>
<li>Columns = calendar quarters; period ends are rounded to the nearest month end</li>
<li><b>*</b> on a quarter = revenue and net income derived as annual (10-K) minus nine-month year-to-date</li>
<li>Cash flows are filed year-to-date, so every quarter's OCF and capex = YTD minus the prior YTD</li>
<li>— = not filed; airlines, cable, and some others report no gross-profit line</li>
<li>Click a quarter (in the chart or the table) to open that 10-Q/10-K on sec.gov</li>
<li>Switch view: tabs or Ctrl+↑ / Ctrl+↓</li>
<li>Switch company: dropdown, ◀ ▶, or Ctrl+← / Ctrl+→</li>
<li>Scroll the table sideways: ← / →</li>
<li>Source: SEC EDGAR XBRL company facts (data.sec.gov); updated 2026-10-09 22:34</li>
<li>Stock price: live chart embedded from TradingView (weekly, last 5 years); no price data is stored on this site</li>
</ul>

  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const sel = $("rt-sel"), box = $("rt-chart"), tip = $("rt-tip");
  const ratio = (a, b) => (a == null || b == null || b === 0) ? null : a / b;
  const fcf = q => (q.ocf == null || q.capex == null) ? null : q.ocf - q.capex;
  const pct = v => v == null ? "—" : (v * 100).toFixed(1) + "%";
  const x2 = v => v == null ? "—" : v.toFixed(2);
  const usd = v => v == null ? "—" : (v < 0 ? "−$" : "$") + Math.abs(v / 1e6).toLocaleString(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 0 }) + "M";
  const shr = v => v == null ? "—" : v >= 1e9 ? (v / 1e9).toFixed(2) + "B" : (v / 1e6).toFixed(1) + "M";
  const VIEWS = [
    { id: "margin", label: "Profit margins", kind: "line", fmt: pct, series: [
      { name: "Gross profit margin", color: "var(--s1)", f: q => ratio(q.gp, q.rev) },
      { name: "Net profit margin", color: "var(--s2)", f: q => ratio(q.ni, q.rev) }] },
    { id: "cr", label: "Current ratio", kind: "line", fmt: x2, ref: 1, series: [
      { name: "Current ratio", color: "var(--s1)", f: q => ratio(q.ca, q.cl) }] },
    { id: "ocf", label: "Net income vs OCF", kind: "line", fmt: usd, series: [
      { name: "Net income", color: "var(--s2)", f: q => q.ni },
      { name: "Operating cash flow", color: "var(--s1)", f: q => q.ocf }] },
    { id: "fcf", label: "Free cash flow", fmt: usd, panels: [
      { title: "Operating cash flow and capex", kind: "bar", h: 230, series: [
        { name: "Operating cash flow", color: "var(--s1)", f: q => q.ocf },
        { name: "Capex", color: "var(--s2)", f: q => q.capex }] },
      { title: "Free cash flow = OCF − capex", kind: "line", h: 200, series: [
        { name: "Free cash flow", color: "var(--s3)", f: fcf }] }] },
    { id: "sh", label: "Shares outstanding", kind: "line", fmt: shr, series: [
      { name: "Shares outstanding", color: "var(--s1)", f: q => splitAdj ? q.shares : q.shares_raw }] },
    { id: "pf", label: "Public float", kind: "bar", fmt: usd, series: [
      { name: "Public float", color: "var(--s1)", f: q => q.float }] },
    { id: "px", label: "Stock price", widget: true },
  ];
  const COLS = [
    ["Revenue", q => usd(q.rev)], ["Gross profit", q => usd(q.gp)],
    ["GPM", q => pct(ratio(q.gp, q.rev)), "margin"], ["Net income", q => usd(q.ni), "ocf"],
    ["NPM", q => pct(ratio(q.ni, q.rev)), "margin"], ["OCF", q => usd(q.ocf), "ocf fcf"],
    ["Capex", q => usd(q.capex), "fcf"], ["FCF", q => usd(fcf(q)), "fcf"],
    ["Current assets", q => usd(q.ca), "cr"], ["Current liab.", q => usd(q.cl), "cr"],
    ["Current ratio", q => x2(ratio(q.ca, q.cl)), "cr"],
    ["Shares out.", q => shr(splitAdj ? q.shares : q.shares_raw), "sh"], ["Public float", q => usd(q.float), "pf"],
  ];
  let ci = 0, vi = 0, splitAdj = true;

  let group = null, og = null;
  DATA.forEach((c, i) => {
    if (c.group !== group) { group = c.group; og = document.createElement("optgroup"); og.label = group; sel.appendChild(og); }
    const o = document.createElement("option"); o.value = i; o.textContent = c.ticker; og.appendChild(o);
  });
  VIEWS.forEach((v, i) => { const b = document.createElement("button"); b.textContent = v.label;
    b.addEventListener("click", () => { vi = i; render(); }); $("rt-tabs").appendChild(b); });

  const NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
  const mk = (svg, tag, a, text) => { const e = document.createElementNS(NS, tag);
    for (const k in a) e.setAttribute(k, a[k]); if (text != null) e.textContent = text; svg.appendChild(e); return e; };
  function niceStep(span) {
    const raw = span / 5, p = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(raw)));
    return [1, 2, 2.5, 5, 10].map(m => m * p).find(s => s >= raw);
  }
  function axisFmt(fmt, v, step) {
    if (fmt === pct) return (v * 100).toFixed(step < .01 ? 1 : 0) + "%";
    if (fmt === x2) return v.toFixed(step < .1 ? 2 : 1);
    if (fmt === shr) return v >= 1e9 || step >= 1e9 ? (v / 1e9).toFixed(step < 1e9 ? 1 : 0) + "B" : (v / 1e6).toFixed(step < 1e6 ? 1 : 0) + "M";
    const a = Math.abs(v), s = v < 0 ? "−$" : "$";
    return a >= 1e9 ? s + (a / 1e9).toFixed(step < 1e9 ? 1 : 0) + "B" : s + (a / 1e6).toFixed(0) + "M";
  }
  const W = 760, L = 58, R = 16, T = 22, B = 30;

  // One panel = one SVG with its own y-axis; the panels of a view share the x-axis.
  function panel(qs, view, p, all) {
    const H = p.h || 290, svg = mk(box, "svg", { viewBox: `0 0 ${W} ${H}` }), el = (...a) => mk(svg, ...a);
    const S = p.S, vals = S.flatMap(s => s.v).filter(v => v != null), n = qs.length, slot = (W - L - R) / n;
    const x = k => L + slot * (k + .5);
    if (p.title) el("text", { x: L, y: 11, "font-weight": 700, opacity: .8 }, p.title);
    if (!vals.length) { el("text", { x: W / 2, y: H / 2, "text-anchor": "middle", opacity: .6 }, "Not filed for this company"); return null; }
    let lo = Math.min(view.ref != null ? view.ref : 0, 0, ...vals), hi = Math.max(view.ref || 0, 0, ...vals);
    if (view.id === "cr") lo = 0;
    if (hi - lo === 0) hi = lo + 1;
    const step = niceStep(hi - lo);
    lo = Math.floor(lo / step) * step; hi = Math.ceil(hi / step) * step;
    const y = v => T + (hi - v) / (hi - lo) * (H - T - B);
    for (let v = lo; v <= hi + step / 1e6; v += step) {
      const zero = Math.abs(v) < step / 1e6;
      el("line", { x1: L, x2: W - R, y1: y(v), y2: y(v), stroke: zero ? "currentColor" : "rgba(128,128,128,.22)", opacity: zero ? .55 : 1 });
      el("text", { x: L - 6, y: y(v) + 4, "text-anchor": "end", opacity: .7 }, axisFmt(view.fmt, v, step));
    }
    if (view.ref != null) {
      el("line", { x1: L, x2: W - R, y1: y(view.ref), y2: y(view.ref), stroke: "currentColor", opacity: .5, "stroke-dasharray": "4 4" });
      el("text", { x: W - R, y: y(view.ref) - 4, "text-anchor": "end", opacity: .6 }, "1.0");
    }
    const every = Math.ceil(n / 12);  // about 12 x labels, always including the latest quarter
    qs.forEach((q, k) => { if ((n - 1 - k) % every === 0)
      el("text", { x: x(k), y: H - 10, "text-anchor": "middle", opacity: .7 }, q.q + (q.derived ? "*" : "")); });
    const marks = [], kindOf = s => s.kind || p.kind || view.kind;
    const bars = S.filter(s => kindOf(s) === "bar"), lines = S.filter(s => kindOf(s) !== "bar");
    // Bars side by side within a quarter (OCF next to capex); lines draw on top.
    const bw = Math.min(24, slot * .72 / Math.max(1, bars.length));
    bars.forEach((s, j) => { const off = (j - (bars.length - 1) / 2) * bw;
      marks.push(s.v.map((v, k) => v == null ? null : el("rect", { x: x(k) + off - bw / 2 + 1, y: Math.min(y(v), y(0)), width: bw - 2,
        height: Math.max(1, Math.abs(y(v) - y(0))), rx: 3, fill: s.color, opacity: v < 0 ? .55 : 1 }))); });
    lines.forEach(s => {
      let d = "", pen = false;
      s.v.forEach((v, k) => { if (v == null) { pen = false; return; } d += (pen ? "L" : "M") + x(k) + " " + y(v); pen = true; });
      el("path", { d, fill: "none", stroke: s.color, "stroke-width": 2, "stroke-linejoin": "round" });
      marks.push(s.v.map((v, k) => v == null ? null : el("circle", { cx: x(k), cy: y(v), r: 4, fill: s.color,
        stroke: "var(--background, #fff)", "stroke-width": 2 })));
    });
    // Direct labels on the latest point (every bar for the sparse annual public float),
    // nudged apart when two end close together. Grouped bars leave values to the tooltip.
    const labels = [...(bars.length > 1 ? [] : bars.map(s => ({ s, off: 0, bar: true }))),
                    ...lines.map(s => ({ s, off: -8, bar: false }))]
      .flatMap(({ s, off, bar }) => (view.id === "pf" ? s.v.map((v, k) => ({ v, k })) : [{ v: s.v[n - 1], k: n - 1 }])
        .map(l => ({ ...l, off, bar })))
      .filter(l => l.v != null)
      .map(l => ({ ...l, ly: l.bar && l.v < 0 ? y(l.v) + 14 : y(l.v) - 9 }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.k - b.k || a.ly - b.ly);
    labels.forEach((l, j) => { const q = labels[j - 1]; if (q && q.k === l.k && l.ly - q.ly < 13) l.ly = q.ly + 13; });
    labels.forEach(l => el("text", { x: x(l.k) + l.off, y: l.ly, "text-anchor": l.bar ? "middle" : "end", "font-weight": 700 }, view.fmt(l.v)));
    const cross = el("line", { y1: T, y2: H - B, stroke: "currentColor", opacity: 0, "stroke-dasharray": "3 3" });
    const hit = el("rect", { x: L, y: 0, width: W - L - R, height: H, fill: "transparent" });
    const hover = k => {
      if (k != null) { cross.setAttribute("x1", x(k)); cross.setAttribute("x2", x(k)); }
      cross.setAttribute("opacity", k == null ? 0 : .4);
      marks.forEach(ms => ms.forEach((m, j) => { if (!m) return;
        if (m.tagName === "circle") m.setAttribute("r", j === k ? 6 : 4); else m.setAttribute("stroke", j === k ? "currentColor" : "none"); })); };
    hit.addEventListener("mousemove", ev => {
      const r = svg.getBoundingClientRect(), sx = (ev.clientX - r.left) * W / r.width;
      all.hover(Math.max(0, Math.min(n - 1, Math.floor((sx - L) / slot))), svg);
    });
    hit.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => all.hover(null));
    hit.style.cursor = "pointer";
    hit.addEventListener("click", ev => {  // click a quarter to open its 10-Q/10-K
      const r = svg.getBoundingClientRect(), sx = (ev.clientX - r.left) * W / r.width;
      const q = qs[Math.max(0, Math.min(n - 1, Math.floor((sx - L) / slot)))];
      if (q.url) window.open(q.url, "_blank", "noopener");
    });
    return { hover, svg, x, y, S, H };
  }

  // Price chart: TradingView's free embed widget draws it in the visitor's browser
  // from TradingView's servers; this site stores no price data.
  function priceWidget(c) {
    const dark = matchMedia("(prefers-color-scheme: dark)").matches;
    const wrap = document.createElement("div");
    wrap.className = "rt-tv tradingview-widget-container";
    wrap.style.height = "420px";
    wrap.innerHTML = '<div class="tradingview-widget-container__widget" style="height:100%"></div>';
    const s = document.createElement("script");
    s.src = "https://s3.tradingview.com/external-embedding/embed-widget-advanced-chart.js";
    s.async = true;
    s.textContent = JSON.stringify({
      autosize: true, symbol: (c.exchange ? c.exchange + ":" : "") + c.ticker, interval: "W", range: "60M",
      timezone: "America/New_York", theme: dark ? "dark" : "light", style: "1", locale: "en",
      allow_symbol_change: false, hide_side_toolbar: true, save_image: false,
      support_host: "https://www.tradingview.com" });
    wrap.appendChild(s);
    box.appendChild(wrap);
  }

  function chart(c, view) {
    box.querySelectorAll("svg, .rt-tv").forEach(s => s.remove()); tip.style.display = "none";
    if (view.widget) { $("rt-legend").innerHTML = ""; priceWidget(c); return; }
    const qs = c.quarters, panels = (view.panels || [view]).map(p => ({ ...p,
      S: p.series.map(s => ({ ...s, v: qs.map(s.f) }))
        // A gross margin that stops before the latest quarter is a tagging gap, not a trend: drop it.
        .filter(s => view.id === "margin" ? s.v[s.v.length - 1] != null : s.v.some(v => v != null)) }));
    const every = (view.panels || [view]).flatMap(p => p.series), shown = panels.flatMap(p => p.S);
    $("rt-legend").innerHTML = every.length < 2 ? "" : every.map(s => shown.some(t => t.name === s.name)
      ? `<span><i style="background:${s.color}"></i>${s.name}</span>`
      : `<span style="opacity:.55"><i style="background:${s.color};opacity:.4"></i>${s.name}: not filed</span>`).join("");
    let drawn = [];
    const all = { hover: (k, svg) => {
      drawn.forEach(d => d.hover(k));
      if (k == null) { tip.style.display = "none"; return; }
      const q = qs[k], d = drawn.find(d => d.svg === svg);
      tip.innerHTML = `<b>${q.q}${q.derived ? "*" : ""}</b> · period end ${q.end}<br>` +
        shown.map(s => `${s.name} <b>${view.fmt(s.v[k])}</b>`).join("<br>") +
        (view.id === "sh" && q.shares_asof ? `<br>as of ${q.shares_asof} (cover page)${q.shares_adj && splitAdj ? `, split-adjusted (as filed: ${shr(q.shares_raw)})` : ""}` : "") +
        (view.id === "pf" && q.float_asof ? `<br>as of ${q.float_asof}` : "") +
        (q.url ? `<br><span style="opacity:.7">Click to open the filing</span>` : "");
      tip.style.display = "block";
      const b = box.getBoundingClientRect(), r = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
      const ys = d.S.map(s => s.v[k]).filter(v => v != null);
      const px = d.x(k) * r.width / W, py = r.top - b.top + (ys.length ? d.y(Math.max(...ys)) : T) * r.height / d.H;
      tip.style.left = Math.max(0, Math.min(px + 12, b.width - tip.offsetWidth - 4)) + "px";
      tip.style.top = Math.max(0, py - tip.offsetHeight - 10) + "px";
    } };
    drawn = panels.map(p => panel(qs, view, p, all)).filter(Boolean);
  }

  function table(c, view) {
    const qs = c.quarters.slice().reverse();
    $("rt-t").innerHTML = "<thead><tr><th>Quarter</th><th>Period end</th>" +
      COLS.map(([h, , v]) => `<th${(v || "").split(" ").includes(view.id) ? ' class="hl"' : ""}>${h}</th>`).join("") + "</tr></thead><tbody>" +
      qs.map(q => `<tr><td>${q.url ? `<a href="${q.url}" target="_blank" title="Open the filing on sec.gov">${q.q}</a>` : q.q}${q.derived ? "*" : ""}</td><td>${q.end}</td>` + COLS.map(([, f, v]) => {
        const t = f(q), cls = [t.startsWith("−") || t.startsWith("-") ? "neg" : "", (v || "").split(" ").includes(view.id) ? "hl" : ""].join(" ").trim();
        return `<td${cls ? ` class="${cls}"` : ""}>${t}</td>`; }).join("") + "</tr>").join("") + "</tbody>";
  }

  function render() {
    const c = DATA[ci], view = VIEWS[vi];
    sel.value = ci;
    $("rt-name").textContent = c.name;
    $("rt-sub").textContent = c.group + " · FYE " + c.fye + (c.splits.length ? " · splits " + c.splits.join(", ") : "");
    [...$("rt-tabs").children].forEach((b, i) => b.classList.toggle("on", i === vi));
    try { history.replaceState(null, "", "#" + c.ticker + "/" + view.id); } catch (e) {}
    $("rt-split").style.display = view.id === "sh" && c.splits.length ? "inline-block" : "none";
    chart(c, view); table(c, view);
  }

  sel.addEventListener("change", () => { ci = +sel.value; render(); });
  $("rt-split-box").addEventListener("change", e => { splitAdj = e.target.checked; render(); });
  $("rt-prev").addEventListener("click", () => { ci = (ci - 1 + DATA.length) % DATA.length; render(); });
  $("rt-next").addEventListener("click", () => { ci = (ci + 1) % DATA.length; render(); });
  document.addEventListener("keydown", e => {
    const wrap = document.querySelector(".rt-wrap");
    if (!(e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey || e.shiftKey) && (e.key === "ArrowLeft" || e.key === "ArrowRight")
        && !/^(INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(document.activeElement.tagName)
        && wrap.scrollWidth > wrap.clientWidth) {
      e.preventDefault(); wrap.scrollBy({ left: e.key === "ArrowLeft" ? -160 : 160, behavior: "smooth" }); return;
    }
    if (!(e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey)) return;
    const d = { ArrowLeft: [-1, 0], ArrowRight: [1, 0], ArrowUp: [0, -1], ArrowDown: [0, 1] }[e.key];
    if (!d) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    ci = (ci + d[0] + DATA.length) % DATA.length; vi = (vi + d[1] + VIEWS.length) % VIEWS.length; render();
  });
  const [ht, hv] = decodeURIComponent(location.hash.slice(1)).split("/");
  ci = Math.max(0, DATA.findIndex(c => c.ticker === (ht || "").toUpperCase()));
  vi = Math.max(0, VIEWS.findIndex(v => v.id === hv));
  render();
})();
</script>

{% endraw %}
